## C36 Round item host (stage A/B) — due, 2026-09-25

- B3 staged Retake/Remove: C36 discard completion for a pending photo child (media disposal, discard terminal, parent slot clearing), S10 preview/Use Photo/Retake parity, and removal of an awaiting-raw child whose source bytes were lost.
- Writer-temporary crash gap (not a regression): EvidenceBundleStore writes `.immutable-<uuid>.tmp` through a transient `..immutable-<uuid>.tmp.<uuid>.tmp` in the generation root that is neither deferred nor retired; a crash during that write still routes the next launch to maintenance, and a save during it fails the after-save reproof. Needs O_EXCL reserved-leaf creation (protection first) or exact writer-temporary retirement, plus a crash-leftover regression.
- Interrupted-finalization startup method: the expected prepared journal was absent in 36075223924; diagnosis pending the test-only location listing.
- Stage A items still unexecuted: recheck-issue cardinality, cancellation mid-open, continuation after the one-pass chain ends (retried items), and SwiftUI presentation/accessibility of the host stages. Critical-state human review of the Round Continue → Begin → photos → outcome → finish journey precedes merge.

## Found due during batch E preparation (2026-09-25)

- C55-1: `S6_4AtomicRestoreTests` Clone/Fork report preservation cannot be corrected test-only. Production re-encodes legacy report snapshots carrying temporal links or assurance on Clone/Fork (`BackupRestoreService` rebinding), and no production producer writes such snapshots through the finalizer. It needs a real producer and an immutability decision, together with the owner's pending `C55_REVERSAL_RESTORE_DECISION.md`. The test's schema pins (33/32) are stale against export 53/52.
- Startup crash-like uncommitted finalization: a prepared journal and promoted snapshot surviving into startup must be retained by private retirement, converge once and resume without refinalizing. No test covers this yet.
- P04-C43 provisional card tooling (`Scripts/v23/p04_c43_contracts.py`, tooling manifests) hashes files that signoff batch 1 changes and names a UI-test path that no longer exists. It is provenance, not a gate, and is recorded as stale rather than rewritten.
- C57-1: the production Replace caller passes no replacement bindings, so a populated cross-workspace My Day Replace stays fail-closed until C57-2 derives bindings at the root. A store-wide `rowID` collision guard is optional hardening.
- Lighting C17/C18 readiness contracts hash the full storage observation, including free bytes (`OfflineReadinessManifestContractsV1` storage SHA fields), so free-space movement between source and snapshot fails with `digestMismatch`. There is no production caller today; correct before those features become reachable.

- PHASE 1 BLOCKER. Backup export self-deadlock. Verified from `sample` stacks and lsof.
  - `BackupExportService.export` → `withCheckRunnerPhotoPublication` → `StaleWriterFenceV1.withAuthorizedCommit` holds the exclusive generation lock (flock).
  - Under it, `validateFrozenCanonical` → `currentStreamingWorkspaceIdentity` builds a fresh `StoreGenerationFactory`, whose new `GenerationLeaseRegistryV1` does a blocking flock on a second descriptor of the same `mutation.lock`. The thread waits forever for its own lock.
  - Introduced in 97360687. Any export that gets past the archive write hangs. Fix in progress: reuse the open authority, with no change to lock code.
- POSSIBLE RELEASE BLOCKER. Nested semantic-export digests in aggregate migration.
  - Each `semanticExportVn` embeds the canonical bytes of Vn-1 as base64 and hashes the whole thing, so cost grows roughly exponentially per layer.
  - A v4 fixture reached 2.36 GB by v49 (V10_01 testV4Accepts…; likely also its 674 s sibling).
  - S10 shipped schema 1.0.0, so the real S10 → V53 upgrade cost is being measured before any proposal.
  - Digests are persisted, so a fix is a restricted migration/digest-format change.
